  "content": "# Code Changes for Hybrid Zoom Strategy (Option C)\n\n## Strategy\n- **Zoom 1-10:** Query pre-computed clusters (fast, few markers)\n- **Zoom 11-18:** Query zoom=0 markers (spatial filter makes it fast for small viewports)\n\n## Changes Needed\n\n### 1. Add Hybrid Zoom Mapping Function\n\nAdd this function to `safecast-new-map.go` (near line 100-200):\n\n```go\n// mapClientZoomToDBZoom maps client zoom to database zoom for hybrid strategy.\n//\n// Strategy:\n//   - Client zoom 0-10: Use pre-computed cluster zoom levels (1-10)\n//   - Client zoom 11+: Use zoom=0 (original markers, fast via spatial index)\n//\n// At high zoom (11+), viewport is small (~1-10 km²), so even though we query\n// zoom=0 (133M markers), the spatial index efficiently filters to just the\n// thousands of markers in the small viewport. This is FAST.\n//\n// At low zoom (1-10), viewport is huge (continents/countries), so we MUST use\n// pre-computed clusters to avoid scanning millions of markers.\nfunc mapClientZoomToDBZoom(clientZoom int) int {\n\t// High zoom (street/building level): use original zoom=0 markers\n\t// Spatial filter on small viewport is fast even with 133M markers\n\tif clientZoom >= 11 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\n\t// Low zoom (continent/country/region): use pre-computed clusters\n\t// Direct mapping: client zoom = db zoom (1-10)\n\tif clientZoom < 1 {\n\t\treturn 1  // Minimum cluster zoom\n\t}\n\n\treturn clientZoom  // 1-10: use corresponding cluster zoom level\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Update First Handler (~line 8064)\n\n**BEFORE:**\n```go\nconst rawZoom = 0\n```\n\n**AFTER:**\n```go\n// Get client zoom from query parameter (default to 7)\nclientZoom := 7\nif zoomStr := r.URL.Query().Get(\"zoom\"); zoomStr != \"\" {\n\tif z, err := strconv.Atoi(zoomStr); err == nil && z >= 0 && z <= 18 {\n\t\tclientZoom = z\n\t}\n}\n\n// Map to database zoom using hybrid strategy\ndbZoom := mapClientZoomToDBZoom(clientZoom)\n```\n\n**Then replace all `rawZoom` with `dbZoom` in this handler.**\n\n### 3. Update Second Handler (~line 8380)\n\nSame change as #2 - replace `const rawZoom = 0` with the client zoom logic.\n\n### 4. Update map.html - Add Zoom Parameter\n\nFind the API call to `/api/markers` and add zoom parameter:\n\n**Find something like:**\n```javascript\nconst url = `/api/markers?minLat=${minLat}&minLon=${minLon}&maxLat=${maxLat}&maxLon=${maxLon}...`;\n```\n\n**Change to:**\n```javascript\nconst zoom = map.getZoom();  // Get current map zoom level\nconst url = `/api/markers?zoom=${zoom}&minLat=${minLat}&minLon=${minLon}&maxLat=${maxLat}&maxLon=${maxLon}...`;\n```\n\n## Why This Works\n\n### Low Zoom (1-10) - Continental/Country View\n- **Viewport:** Huge (1000+ km across)\n- **Without clusters:** Query would return millions of markers → SLOW\n- **With clusters:** Query returns 10-1000 clusters → FAST (50-100ms)\n\n### High Zoom (11-18) - Street/Building View\n- **Viewport:** Small (1-10 km across)\n- **Spatial filter:** Efficiently narrows 133M markers to just thousands in viewport\n- **Result:** FAST (50-200ms) even querying zoom=0\n\n**Example (Tokyo at zoom 15):**\n- Viewport: ~2km × 2km\n- Spatial index filters: 133M markers → ~5,000 in viewport\n- Query time: ~100ms (spatial index is efficient!)\n\n## Expected Performance\n\n| Client Zoom | DB Zoom | What It Queries | Markers Returned | Time |\n|-------------|---------|-----------------|------------------|------|\n| 0-5 | 1-5 | Clusters | 1-100 | ~20ms |\n| 6-7 | 6-7 | Clusters | 100-500 | ~30ms |\n| 8-10 | 8-10 | Clusters | 500-5,000 | ~50ms |\n| 11-13 | 0 | Original (spatially filtered) | 1,000-10,000 | ~100ms |\n| 14-18 | 0 | Original (spatially filtered) | 100-1,000 | ~50ms |\n\n**Overall: 50-100x improvement over current (7800ms → 50-100ms)**\n\n## Testing\n\nAfter applying changes and rebuilding:\n\n```bash\n# Test low zoom (should use clusters)\ncurl \"http://localhost:8765/api/markers?zoom=7&minLat=35&maxLat=36&minLon=139&maxLon=140\"\n# Should be FAST (cluster query)\n\n# Test high zoom (should use zoom=0 with spatial filter)\ncurl \"http://localhost:8765/api/markers?zoom=15&minLat=35.6&maxLat=35.7&minLon=139.6&maxLon=139.7\"\n# Should be FAST (small viewport, spatial index works great)\n```\n\n## Rollback\n\nIf needed:\n1. Revert code changes (`const rawZoom = 0`)\n2. System still works (zoom=0 markers unchanged)\n3. Delete cluster markers: `DELETE FROM markers WHERE zoom > 0 AND marker_count > 1;`\n"
}

  8428→		baseSrc, errCh = streamMultipleTracks(ctx, trackIDs, rawZoom, minLat, minLon, maxLat, maxLon, *dbType)
  8429→	} else {
  8430→		baseSrc, errCh = db.StreamMarkersByZoomAndBounds(ctx, rawZoom, minLat, minLon, maxLat, maxLon, *dbType)
  8431→	}
  8432→
  8433→	// Fetch current realtime points once so the map reflects network devices.
  8434→	// We only touch the realtime table when the dedicated flag enables it so
  8435→	// operators control the feature explicitly.
  8436→	var rtMarks []database.Marker
  8437→	if *safecastRealtimeEnabled {
  8438→		var err error
  8439→		rtMarks, err = db.GetLatestRealtimeByBounds(ctx, minLat, minLon, maxLat, maxLon, *dbType)

  8441→			log.Printf("realtime query: %v", err)
  8442→		}
  8443→		// Log bounds alongside count to help diagnose empty map tiles.
  8444→		log.Printf("realtime markers: %d lat[%f,%f] lon[%f,%f]", len(rtMarks), minLat, maxLat, minLon, maxLon)
  8445→	}
  8446→
  8447→	// Apply density-based sampling if needed (zoom < 8)
  8448→	var sampledSrc <-chan database.Marker
  8449→	if sampleRate < 1.0 {
  8450→		sampledSrc = sampleMarkerChannel(ctx, baseSrc, sampleRate)
  8451→	} else {
  8452→		sampledSrc = baseSrc
